\frac{1}{2}\langle\hat{r}_{j}(\frac{d}{dt}\hat{r}_{k})+(\frac{d}{dt}\hat{r}_{k})\hat{r}_{j}\rangle-\langle\hat{r}_{j}\rangle\langle\frac{d}{dt}\hat{r}_{k}\rangle
,
\frac{d}{dt}\hat{r}_{k}
is replaced by the HLE, while
\hat{r}_{j}(t)
is replaced by the formal solution to its HLE. We assume that the initial-time value of
\hat{r}_{j}(t)
is uncorrelated with the input-field,
\frac{1}{2}\langle\hat{r}_{j}(t_{0})\hat{r}_{k,\mathrm{in}}(t)+\hat{r}_{k,\mathrm{in}}(t)\hat{r}_{j}(t_{0})\rangle-\langle\hat{r}_{j}(t_{0})\rangle\langle\hat{r}_{k,\mathrm{in}}(t)\rangle=0
, while the same-time correlation between the system and environment is calculated to be
\frac{1}{2}\langle\hat{r}_{j}(t)\hat{r}_{k,\mathrm{in}}(t)+\hat{r}_{k,\mathrm{in}}(t)\hat{r}_{j}(t)\rangle-\langle\hat{r}_{j}(t)\rangle\langle\hat{r}_{k,\mathrm{in}}(t)\rangle=\frac{1}{2}(\Omega_{2N}H_{\mathrm{sb}})_{jl}(\bar{S}_{\mathrm{in}})_{lk}
. Combining terms then yields the following expressions for the intracavity moments:

\frac{d}{dt}\bm{\mu}=\bm{A}\bm{\mu}+\bm{\Omega}_{2N}(\bm{h}_{\mathbf{s}}+\bm{H}_{\mathrm{sb}}\bm{\mu}_{\mathrm{in}}),\qquad\frac{d}{dt}\bm{\Sigma}=\bm{A}\bm{\Sigma}+\bm{\Sigma}\bm{A}^{T}-\bm{\Omega}_{2N}\bm{H}_{\mathrm{sb}}\bar{\bm{S}}_{\mathrm{in}}\bm{H}_{\mathrm{sb}}^{T}\bm{\Omega}_{2N}.

(57)

In order for these dynamical equations to match those of the intracavity pointer states generated from the Lindblad master equation, represented by the arrays in Eq. (
52
), it is required that
\bm{H}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}}+s\bm{H}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}}=\bm{H}_{\mathrm{s}}
and
\bm{h}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}}+s\bm{h}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}}=\bm{h}_{\mathbf{s}}+\bm{H}_{\mathrm{sb}}\bm{\mu}_{\mathrm{in}}
. Additionally, the matrix of decay rates is related to the input-noise correlators and system-bath coupling rates via
\bm{\Gamma}=\bm{H}_{\mathrm{sb}}(\bar{\bm{S}}_{\mathrm{in}}+i\bm{\Omega}_{2M}/2)\bm{H}_{\mathrm{sb}}^{T}
. The input fields obey the Robertson-Schrödinger uncertainty relation if
\bar{\bm{S}}_{\mathrm{in}}+i\bm{\Omega}_{2M}/2\geq 0
, and so it follows that
\bm{\Gamma}\geq 0
. Since
\bm{\Gamma}
is Hermitian and positive semidefinite, the dynamics of the equivalent Lindblad master equation will be completely positive and trace-preserving. This relation also demonstrates that
\bm{H}_{\mathrm{sb}}
is not uniquely determined by
\bm{\Gamma}
and
\bar{\bm{S}}_{\mathrm{in}}
, and hence a Lindblad master equation does not correspond to a unique set of HLEs.

A.4
Input-output theory and scattering

Following standard input-output theory, the HLEs in Eq. (
54
) may also be written in time-reversed form using output-field quadrature operators,
\hat{r}_{\mathrm{out},j}(t)\vcentcolon=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\left(\exp[-i\omega(t-t_{1})\bm{\Omega}]\right)_{jk}\hat{r}_{\mathrm{b},k}(\omega;t_{1})\,d\omega/\sqrt{2\pi}
, where
\hat{r}_{\mathrm{b},k}(\omega;t_{1})
is the value of
\hat{r}_{\mathrm{b},k}(\omega)
at some future time
t_{1}>t
. By equating both the time-forward and reversed HLEs, the input-output relation is obtained:

\hat{\bm{r}}_{\mathrm{out}}(t)-\hat{\bm{r}}_{\mathrm{in}}(t)=\bm{\Omega}_{2M}\bm{H}_{\mathrm{sb}}^{T}\hat{\bm{r}}(t).

(58)

To derive a scattering relation between the input and output fields, the HLEs in Eq. (
54
) are first Fourier-transformed, yielding a set of linear equations in frequency space. Solving these linear equations and then substituting them into the Fourier-transformed input-output relation Eq. (
58
) produces the following scattering equation:

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{r}}_{\mathrm{out}}[\omega]=\mathbf{S}[\omega]\hat{\bm{r}}_{\mathrm{in}}[\omega]+\mathbf{T}[\omega]\quad\text{where}\quad\mathbf{S}[\omega]

\displaystyle=-\bm{\Omega}_{2M}\bm{H}_{\mathrm{sb}}^{T}\left(\bm{A}+i\omega\bm{I}_{2N}\right)^{-1}\!\bm{\Omega}_{2N}\bm{H}_{\mathrm{sb}}+\bm{I}_{2N}

(59)

\displaystyle\mathbf{T}[\omega]

\displaystyle=-\bm{\Omega}_{2M}\bm{H}_{\mathrm{sb}}^{T}\left(\bm{A}+i\omega\bm{I}_{2N}\right)^{-1}\!\bm{\Omega}_{2N}\bm{h}_{\mathrm{s}}.

We have defined the Fourier-transformed operators as
\hat{r}_{j}[\omega]\vcentcolon=\int^{\infty}_{-\infty}e^{i\omega t}\hat{r}_{j}(t)dt/\sqrt{2\pi}
.

In this work, each system mode is coupled to its own bath, so that the number of system modes and baths is the same,
N=M
. Our choice of quadrature basis for the system and bath corresponds to the convention defined in Eq. (
8
), and therefore we use
\hat{\bm{r}}=(\hat{q}_{1},\hat{p}_{1},\ldots,\hat{q}_{N},\hat{p}_{N})
and
\hat{\bm{r}}_{\mathrm{b}}(\omega)=(\hat{q}_{\mathrm{b},1}(\omega),\hat{p}_{\mathrm{b},1}(\omega),\ldots,\hat{q}_{\mathrm{b},N}(\omega),\hat{p}_{\mathrm{b},N}(\omega))
. The bath and system modes are assumed to exchange a conserved number of excitations, i.e. the system-bath couplings take the form of beam-splitter interactions with arbitrary phases:

\hat{H}_{\mathrm{sb}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\sum_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}\in N}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\sqrt{\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}}\Big(\!\cos(\phi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}})\left[\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}\hat{q}_{\mathrm{b},\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}(\omega)+\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}\hat{p}_{\mathrm{b},\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}(\omega)\right]-\sin(\phi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}})\left[\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}\hat{p}_{\mathrm{b},\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}(\omega)-\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}\hat{q}_{\mathrm{b},\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}(\omega)\right]\!\Big)\,d\omega.

(60)

The matrix representing the couplings in
\hat{H}_{\mathrm{sb}}
is therefore
\bm{H}_{\mathrm{sb}}=\bigoplus_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}\in N}\sqrt{\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}}\left(\begin{smallmatrix}\cos(\phi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}})&-\sin(\phi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}})\\
\sin(\phi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}})&\cos(\phi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}})\end{smallmatrix}\right)
. Without loss of generality, all phases are set to
\phi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}=-\pi/2
so that
\bm{\Omega}_{2N}\bm{H}_{\mathrm{sb}}=-\sqrt{\bm{\upgamma}}
where
\bm{\upgamma}=\bigoplus_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}\in N}\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}\bm{I}_{2}
. The Heisenberg-Langevin equations in Eq. (
54
) are now

\frac{d}{dt}\hat{\bm{r}}(t)=\bm{A}\hat{\bm{r}}(t)+\bm{\Omega}_{2N}\bm{h}_{\mathbf{s}}-\sqrt{\bm{\upgamma}}\hat{\bm{r}}_{\mathrm{in}}(t)\quad\text{where}\quad\bm{A}=\bm{\Omega}_{2N}\bm{H}_{\mathrm{s}}-\frac{1}{2}\bm{\upgamma}.

(61)

Consequently, the input-output relation in Eq. (
58
) are
\hat{\bm{r}}_{\mathrm{out}}(t)-\hat{\bm{r}}_{\mathrm{in}}(t)=\sqrt{\bm{\upgamma}}\hat{\bm{r}}(t)
. Finally, the matrices in the scattering equation Eq. (
59
) simplify to

\mathbf{S}[\omega]=\sqrt{\bm{\upgamma}}\left(\bm{A}+i\omega\bm{I}_{2N}\right)^{-1}\!\!\!\!\sqrt{\bm{\upgamma}}+\bm{I}_{2N},\qquad\mathbf{T}[\omega]=\sqrt{\bm{\upgamma}}\left(\bm{A}+i\omega\bm{I}_{2N}\right)^{-1}\!\bm{\Omega}_{2N}\bm{h}_{\mathrm{s}},

(62)

Provided that each input field is in a thermal state, then the input covariance matrix is
\bar{\bm{S}}_{\mathrm{in}}=\bigoplus_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}\in N}(n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}+1/2)\bm{I}_{2}
. As a result, the dissipation matrix in the corresponding Lindbladian takes the form
\bm{\Gamma}=\sqrt{\bm{\upgamma}}(\bar{\bm{S}}_{\mathrm{in}}+i\bm{\Omega}_{2N}/2)\sqrt{\bm{\upgamma}}=\bigoplus_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}\in N}\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}((n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}+1/2)\bm{I}_{2}+i\bm{\Omega}_{2}/2)
.

Appendix B
Reproducing expressions for dephasing in simple systems

B.1
A single mode in a coherent state

Here we calculate the qubit dephasing rate in the case of a single mode (labeled the C mode) in a coherent state with qubit state-dependent amplitudes. Using the formalism developed in Sec.
II
, we show that the resulting dephasing rate is equivalent to Eq. (5.20) in Ref.
Gambetta et al. 2006
. We begin with the following Lindbladian, written in the rotating frame:

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\hat{\rho})=-i[{\hat{H}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}}+\hat{\sigma}_{z}\hat{H}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}}},{\hat{\rho}}]

\displaystyle+\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\mathcal{D}[\hat{c}](\hat{\rho})

\displaystyle=-i[{\hat{H}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}}+\hat{\sigma}_{z}\hat{H}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}}},{\hat{\rho}}]

\displaystyle+\frac{\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}{2}\left(\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\hat{\rho}\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}-\frac{1}{2}\{{\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}},{\hat{\rho}}\}+\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\hat{\rho}\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}-\frac{1}{2}\{{\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}},{\hat{\rho}}\}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{i\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}{2}\left(\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\hat{\rho}\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}-\frac{1}{2}\{{\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}},{\hat{\rho}}\}-\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\hat{\rho}\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}+\frac{1}{2}\{{\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}},{\hat{\rho}}\}\right),

(63)

where the Hamiltonian terms consist of a drive with complex strength
\varepsilon
and detuning
\Delta
, as well as the dispersive interaction between the cavity mode and qubit:

\displaystyle\hat{H}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}}

\displaystyle=-\Delta\hat{c}^{\dagger}\hat{c}+\varepsilon\hat{c}^{\dagger}+\varepsilon^{*}\hat{c}=-\frac{\Delta}{2}\left(\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}+\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}-1\right)+\sqrt{2}\left(\real[\varepsilon]\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}+\mathrm{Im}[\varepsilon]\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\right),

(64)

\displaystyle\hat{H}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}}

\displaystyle=\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\hat{c}^{\dagger}\hat{c}=\frac{\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}{2}\left(\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}+\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}-1\right).

(65)

Using Eqs. (
12
) and (
13
), we construct the following coefficient matrices in the basis
\hat{\bm{r}}=(\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}},\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}})
:

\bm{H}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}}=-\Delta\bm{I},\qquad\bm{h}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}}=\sqrt{2}\begin{pmatrix}\real[\varepsilon]&\mathrm{Im}[\varepsilon]\end{pmatrix}^{T},\qquad\bm{H}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}}=\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\bm{I},\qquad h_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}}=-\frac{\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}{2},\qquad\bm{\Gamma}=\frac{\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}{2}\left(\bm{I}+i\bm{J}\right),

(66)

where the
2\times 2
matrices
\bm{I}
and
\bm{J}
are as follows:

\bm{I}\vcentcolon=\begin{pmatrix}1&0\\
0&1\end{pmatrix},\qquad\bm{J}\vcentcolon=\begin{pmatrix}0&1\\
-1&0\end{pmatrix}.

(67)

In addition,
\bm{\Omega}=\bm{J}
for this single mode. The matrices necessary to solve for the moments of the system may then be constructed using Eq. (
II.2
):

\bm{A}=-\frac{\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}{2}\bm{I}-\Delta\bm{J},\qquad\bm{B}=2i\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\bm{I},\qquad\bm{C}=\frac{1}{2}\left(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}+i\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\right)\bm{I},\qquad\bm{d}=\sqrt{2}\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{Im}[\varepsilon]&-\real[\varepsilon]\end{pmatrix}^{T}.

(68)

Recalling that
\bm{\Sigma}=\bm{\Sigma}^{T}
, these matrices generate the following differential equations for the first and second moments:

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mu_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}

\displaystyle=-\left(2i\chi\Sigma_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}+\frac{\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}{2}\right)\mu_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}-\left(2i\chi\Sigma_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}+\Delta\right)\mu_{p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}+\sqrt{2}\mathrm{Im}[\varepsilon],

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mu_{p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}

\displaystyle=-\left(2i\chi\Sigma_{p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}+\frac{\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}{2}\right)\mu_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}-\left(2i\chi\Sigma_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}-\Delta\right)\mu_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}-\sqrt{2}\real[\varepsilon],

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\Sigma_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}

\displaystyle=-2\Delta\Sigma_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}-\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\Sigma_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}-2i\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}(\Sigma_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}^{2}+\Sigma_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}^{2})+\frac{1}{2}(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}+i\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}),

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\Sigma_{p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}

\displaystyle=2\Delta\Sigma_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}-\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\Sigma_{p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}-2i\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}(\Sigma_{p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}^{2}+\Sigma_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}^{2})+\frac{1}{2}(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}+i\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}),

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\Sigma_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}

\displaystyle=-\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\Sigma_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}-2i\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\Sigma_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}(\Sigma_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}+\Sigma_{p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}).

(69)

We first solve for the covariance in the steady state, yielding multiple solutions for each matrix element. The only valid solutions satisfy
\real[\Sigma_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}],\real[\Sigma_{p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}]>0
as this retains the integrability of
W(\bm{r})
. This results in the expected covariance matrix for a coherent state,
\bm{\Sigma}=\bm{I}/2
, and accordingly, from Eq. (
20
), no parasitic dephasing (
\Gamma_{\mathrm{d,p}}=0
). Plugging the covariance matrix into the equations for the steady state equations for the first moments, we find:

\mu_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}=\sqrt{2}\frac{\left(i\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}+\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}/2\right)\mathrm{Im}[\varepsilon]+\Delta\real[\varepsilon]}{\left(i\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}+\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}/2\right)^{2}+\Delta^{2}},\qquad\mu_{p_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}=-\sqrt{2}\frac{\left(i\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}+\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}/2\right)\real[\varepsilon]+\Delta\mathrm{Im}[\varepsilon]}{\left(i\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}+\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}/2\right)^{2}+\Delta^{2}}.

(70)

Computing the measurement-induced dephasing with Eq. (
21
) then produces:

\Gamma_{\mathrm{d,m}}=\frac{2|\varepsilon|^{2}\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}{\left[\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}/4+(\Delta-\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}})^{2}\right]\left[\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}/4+(\Delta+\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}})^{2}\right]}=\frac{\left(n_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}},e}+n_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}},g}\right)\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}{\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}/4+\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}+\Delta^{2}},\quad\text{where}\quad n_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}},e/g}=\frac{|\varepsilon|^{2}}{\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}/4+\left(\Delta\pm\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\right)^{2}}

(71)

is the qubit state-dependent intracavity occupancy of mode C. This is in agreement with Eqs. (5.20) and (5.21) of Ref.
Gambetta et al. 2006
.

B.2
A single mode in a thermal state

Here we analyze the case of a single mode (labeled the C mode) occupied by a thermal state characterized by the mean photon number
n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}
. In doing so, we reproduce Eq. (44) in Ref.
Clerk and Utami 2007
. We begin with the following Lindbladian, written in the rotating frame:

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\hat{\rho})=-i[{\hat{\sigma}_{z}\hat{H}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}}},{\hat{\rho}}]

\displaystyle+\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}(n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}+1)\mathcal{D}[\hat{c}](\hat{\rho})+\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\mathcal{D}[\hat{c}^{\dagger}](\hat{\rho})\!

\displaystyle=-i[{\hat{\sigma}_{z}\hat{H}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}}},{\hat{\rho}}]

\displaystyle+\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\left(n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\left(\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\hat{\rho}\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}-\frac{1}{2}\{{\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}},{\hat{\rho}}\}+\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\hat{\rho}\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}-\frac{1}{2}\{{\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}},{\hat{\rho}}\}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{i\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}{2}\left(\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\hat{\rho}\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}-\frac{1}{2}\{{\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}},{\hat{\rho}}\}-\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\hat{\rho}\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}+\frac{1}{2}\{{\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}},{\hat{\rho}}\}\right),

(72)

where the
\hat{H}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}}
is given in Eq. (
65
). Using Eqs. (
12
) and (
13
), we can construct the following coefficient matrices in the basis
\hat{\bm{r}}=(\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}},\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}})
:

\bm{H}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}}=\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\bm{I},\qquad h_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{Q}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{R}$}}}=-\frac{\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}{2},\qquad\bm{\Gamma}=\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\left(n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}+\frac{1}{2}\right)\bm{I}+i\frac{\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}{2}\bm{J},

(73)

where the
2\times 2
matrices
\bm{I}
and
\bm{J}
are defined in Eqs. (
67
), and
\bm{\Omega}=\bm{J}
. We then use Eq. (
II.2
) to construct matrices necessary to solve for the second moment of the system:

\bm{A}=-\frac{\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}{2}\bm{I},\qquad\bm{B}=2i\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\bm{I},\qquad\bm{C}=\left[\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\left(n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}+\frac{1}{2}\right)+i\frac{\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}{2}\right]\bm{I}.

(74)

These matrices generate a set of differential equations for the covariance matrix entries. As in the previous subsection, the only physically valid solution results in off-diagonal components which are zero. The diagonal entries therefore have identical dynamical equations of the form

\frac{d}{dt}\Sigma_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}=-2i\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\Sigma_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}^{2}-\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\Sigma_{q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}q_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}+\frac{i\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}{2}+\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\left(n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}+\frac{1}{2}\right),

(75)

which can be solved in the steady state and utilized with Eq. (
20
) to yield

\Gamma_{\mathrm{d,p}}=\frac{\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}{2}\real\left[\sqrt{\left(1+\frac{2i\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}{\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}\right)^{2}+\frac{8i\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}{\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}}-1\right],

(76)

in agreement with Eq. (44) of Ref.
Clerk and Utami 2007
. Expanding
\Gamma_{\mathrm{d,p}}
to first order around
n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}=0
produces the expression in Eq. (
35
).

Appendix C
The embedded amplifier

C.1
Analytic solution for the dephasing

The coefficient arrays for the embedded amplifier described in Sec.
V
are obtained using the Hamiltonian in Eq. (
26
). The arrays
\bm{A}
and
\bm{d}
take the forms

\displaystyle\bm{A}

\displaystyle=\begin{pmatrix}-(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}}/2)\bm{I}+\lambda[-\cos(\theta)\bm{X}+\sin(\theta)\bm{Z}]&g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}\bm{J}&g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}[\cos(\phi)\bm{J}+\sin(\phi)\bm{I}]\\
g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}\bm{J}&-(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}/2)\bm{I}&g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\bm{J}\\
g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}[\cos(\phi)\bm{J}-\sin(\phi)\bm{I}]&g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\bm{J}&-(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}/2)\bm{I}\end{pmatrix},

\displaystyle\bm{d}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{2}\varepsilon\begin{pmatrix}0&0&\sin(\varphi)&-\cos(\varphi)&0&0\end{pmatrix}^{T},

(77)

where the
2\times 2
matrices
\bm{I}
and
\bm{J}
are defined in Eq. (
67
), and
\bm{X}
and
\bm{Z}
are defined as

\bm{X}\vcentcolon=\begin{pmatrix}0&1\\
1&0\end{pmatrix},\qquad\bm{Z}\vcentcolon=\begin{pmatrix}1&0\\
0&-1\end{pmatrix}.

(78)

Thermal noise from the environment is included for each mode. Additionally, to simplify our analysis we assume only mode C to have a non-negligible coupling to the qubit. The other arrays required to solve the moments,
\bm{B}
and
\bm{C}
, are then:

\bm{B}=\begin{pmatrix}\bm{0}&\bm{0}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\bm{0}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\bm{0}&2i\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\bm{I}\end{pmatrix},\qquad\bm{C}=\frac{1}{2}\begin{pmatrix}\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}}(2n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}}+1)\bm{I}&\bm{0}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}(2n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}+1)\bm{I}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\bm{0}&[i\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}+\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}(2n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}+1)]\bm{I}\end{pmatrix}.

(79)

The equation which characterizes the steady-state of the covariance matrix is known as a continuous algebraic Riccati equation (CARE)
Bittanti et al. 1991
;
Lancaster and Rodman 1995
, which may be solved by linearizing the differential equation for
\bm{\Sigma}
:

\frac{d}{dt}\bm{\Sigma}=\bm{A}\bm{\Sigma}+\bm{\Sigma}\bm{A}^{T}-\bm{\Sigma}\bm{B}\bm{\Sigma}+\bm{C}\,\,\rightarrow\,\,\frac{d}{dt}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{U}\\
\bm{V}\end{pmatrix}=\mathbf{H}\begin{pmatrix}\bm{U}\\
\bm{V}\end{pmatrix}\text{ where }\,\mathbf{H}=\begin{pmatrix}\bm{A}^{T}&-\bm{B}\\
-\bm{C}&-\bm{A}\end{pmatrix}.

(80)

\bm{U}
and
\bm{V}
are both
6\times 6
matrices, and provided that
\bm{U}
is invertible, then the solution for the covariance matrix is
\bm{\Sigma}=\bm{V}\bm{U}^{-1}
. In order for
\bm{V}\bm{U}^{-1}
to be the steady-state solution for the CARE,
(\bm{U}\,\,\bm{V})^{T}
must span the space of stable solutions for
\mathbf{H}
. For simplicity, the eigenvectors corresponding to the stable eigenvalues of
\mathbf{H}
were used as the columns of
(\bm{U}\,\,\bm{V})^{T}
. Tractable analytic solutions are only possible when the loop-phase is
\phi=\pm\pi/2
. In this case, the eigenvalues are independent of the phase of the amplifier
\theta
. This is evident from the characteristic polynomial of
\mathbf{H}
, which may be expressed as

0=\Big(s^{6}+v_{2}(+\lambda)s^{4}+v_{1}(+\lambda)s^{2}+v_{0}\bm{(}+\lambda,\mathrm{sgn}(\phi)\bm{)}\Big)\Big(s^{6}+v_{2}(-\lambda)s^{4}+v_{1}(-\lambda)s^{2}+v_{0}\bm{(}-\lambda,\mathrm{sgn}(\phi)\bm{)}\Big).

(81)

The twelve eigenvalues of
\mathbf{H}
have the form
\pm\sqrt{\mathrm{r}_{k}(\pm\lambda)}\,,\,k=1,2,3
, where
\mathrm{r}_{k}(\pm\lambda)
are the roots of the cubic polynomial
0=s^{3}+v_{2}(\pm\lambda)s^{2}+v_{1}(\pm\lambda)s+v_{0}\bm{(}\pm\lambda,\mathrm{sgn}(\phi)\bm{)}
. Six of these eigenvalues have a negative real part, and so their associated eigenvectors form the stable subspace of
\mathbf{H}
, and hence the columns of
(\bm{U}\,\,\bm{V})^{T}
. From here,
\bm{\Sigma}=\bm{V}\bm{U}^{-1}
may be solved, followed by
\bm{\mu}
. Using Eq. (
20
), the parasitic dephasing is then:

\Gamma_{\mathrm{d,p}}=-\frac{1}{2}(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}}+\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}+\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}})+\frac{1}{2}\!\sum_{k=1,2,3}\hskip-2.84526pt\mathrm{Re}\!\left[\sqrt{\mathrm{r}_{k}(+\lambda)}+\sqrt{\mathrm{r}_{k}(-\lambda)}\right].

(82)

The measurement induced dephasing is also obtained using Eq. (
21
):

\displaystyle\Gamma_{\mathrm{d,m}}=\varepsilon^{2}\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}\Bigg(

\displaystyle\frac{u_{1}\bm{(}+\lambda,\mathrm{sgn}(\phi)\bm{)}\big[\mathrm{sgn}(\phi)g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}-g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}}/2+\lambda)\big]^{2}}{\left\lvert{\mathrm{r}_{1}(+\lambda)\,\mathrm{r}_{2}(+\lambda)\,\mathrm{r}_{3}(+\lambda)}\right\rvert^{2}}\big(1-\sin(\theta-2\varphi)\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{u_{1}\bm{(}-\lambda,\mathrm{sgn}(\phi)\bm{)}\big[\mathrm{sgn}(\phi)g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}-g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}}/2-\lambda)\big]^{2}}{\left\lvert{\mathrm{r}_{1}(-\lambda)\,\mathrm{r}_{2}(-\lambda)\,\mathrm{r}_{3}(-\lambda)}\right\rvert^{2}}\big(1+\sin(\theta-2\varphi)\big)\Bigg)

(83)

where
\left\lvert{\mathrm{r}_{1}(\lambda)\,\mathrm{r}_{2}(\lambda)\,\mathrm{r}_{3}(\lambda)}\right\rvert^{2}=\left\lvert{v_{0}\bm{(}\lambda,\mathrm{sgn}(\phi)\bm{)}}\right\rvert^{2}=\big[u_{0}(\lambda)-\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}u_{2}(\lambda)\big]^{2}+\big[\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}u_{1}\bm{(}\lambda,\mathrm{sgn}(\phi)\bm{)}\big]^{2}
, and

\displaystyle u_{0}(\lambda)=\left[g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}^{2}\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}/2+g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}}/2+\lambda)+g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}/2+(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}}/2+\lambda)(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}/2)(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}/2)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle u_{1}\bm{(}\lambda,\mathrm{sgn}(\phi)\bm{)}=\Big(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}}(2n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}}+1)\left[g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}+\mathrm{sgn}(\phi)g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}/2\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 76.82243pt+\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}(2n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}+1)\left[\mathrm{sgn}(\phi)g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}-g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}}/2+\lambda)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 76.82243pt+\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}(2n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}+1)\left[g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}^{2}+(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}}/2+\lambda)(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}/2)\right]^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle u_{2}(\lambda)=\left[g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}^{2}+(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}}/2+\lambda)(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}/2)\right]^{2}.

(84)

C.2
Amplifier stability and gain

In order for the embedded amplifier to be stable, the eigenvalues of the dynamical matrix for the pointer states must have a negative real part:

\bm{A}=\begin{pmatrix}-(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}}/2)\bm{I}+\lambda[-\cos(\theta)\bm{X}+\sin(\theta)\bm{Z}]&g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}\bm{J}&g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}[\cos(\phi)\bm{J}+\sin(\phi)\bm{I}]\\
g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}\bm{J}&-(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}/2)\bm{I}&g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\bm{J}\\
