g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}[\cos(\phi)\bm{J}-\sin(\phi)\bm{I}]&g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\bm{J}&-(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}/2)\bm{I}+s\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\bm{J}\end{pmatrix},

(85)

where
s=\braket{\sigma|\hat{\sigma}_{z}|\sigma}
for
\sigma=e,g
. The inclusion of qubit detuning results in complicated inequalities when using the Routh-Hurwitz criterion for stability. As a result, we instead focus on the critical criteria for stability
Fuller 1968
, where one or both of
\det[\bm{A}]
and the 5
th
Hurwitz determinant go exactly to zero on the border between the regions of stability and instability, and are otherwise non-zero. Applying the nonreciprocity condition from Eq. (
34
), we find that

\det[\bm{A}]\propto(1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}-\mathcal{C}_{\lambda})(1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}+\mathcal{C}_{\lambda})[(1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}})^{2}+\mathcal{X}^{2}](\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}}\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}})^{2}
, which goes to zero exactly when
\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}=1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}
. Hence, Eq. (
37
) is a sufficient but not necessary criterion to mark the transition from stability to instability for the embedded amplifier.

We can compute the scattering matrix in the quadrature basis using Eq. (
62
), where the matrix
\bm{A}
is defined in Eq. (
85
) and
\bm{\upgamma}=\mathrm{diag}(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}},\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}},\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}},\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}},\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}},\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}})
. We focus solely on scattering on resonance where the nonreciprocity condition for C
\rightarrow
A has been applied. The scattering matrix may then be written in block form as follows:

\mathbf{S}[0]=\begin{pmatrix}\mathbf{S}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}}&\mathbf{S}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}&\mathbf{S}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\\
\mathbf{S}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}}&\mathbf{S}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}&\mathbf{S}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\\
\bm{0}&\mathbf{S}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}&\mathbf{S}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\end{pmatrix}=-\mathbf{S}_{\mathrm{sqz}}\mathbf{S}_{\mathrm{bs}}.

(86)

To simplify the expression for the total scattering of the embedded amplifier,
\mathbf{S}[0]
may be decomposed into a product of two scattering matrices:
\mathbf{S}_{\mathrm{sqz}}
, which represents the scattering for modes A and B coupled via a beam splitter with amplification at mode A (where coupling to mode C and the qubit is omitted,
g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}},g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}=0
and
\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}=0
), and
\mathbf{S}_{\mathrm{bs}}
, which corresponds to the scattering between the beam-splitter coupled modes B and C with the qubit detuning present (here, coupling to mode A and the amplification are omitted,
g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}},g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}=0
and
\lambda=0
). These scattering matrices may be written as:

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{\mathrm{sqz}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\Lambda}\begin{pmatrix}\begin{subarray}{c}\textstyle{(1-\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}^{2}+\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}^{2})\bm{I}}\\[1.00006pt]
\textstyle{+\,2\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}[-\cos(\theta)\bm{X}+\sin(\theta)\bm{Z}]}\end{subarray}&\begin{subarray}{c}\textstyle{2\sqrt{\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}}(1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}})\bm{J}}\\[1.00006pt]
\textstyle{+\,2\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}\sqrt{\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}}[\cos(\theta)\bm{Z}+\sin(\theta)\bm{X}]}\end{subarray}&\bm{0}\\[11.99998pt]
\begin{subarray}{c}\textstyle{2\sqrt{\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}}(1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}})\bm{J}}\\[1.00006pt]
\textstyle{-\,2\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}\sqrt{\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}}[\cos(\theta)\bm{Z}+\sin(\theta)\bm{X}]}\end{subarray}&\begin{subarray}{c}\textstyle{(1-\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}^{2}-\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}^{2})\bm{I}}\\[1.00006pt]
\textstyle{+\,2\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}[-\cos(\theta)\bm{X}+\sin(\theta)\bm{Z}]}\end{subarray}&\bm{0}\\[11.99998pt]
\bm{0}&\bm{0}&\Lambda\bm{I}\end{pmatrix}\,\,,\,\,\Lambda=(1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}})^{2}-\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{\mathrm{bs}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\mathrm{X}}\begin{pmatrix}\mathrm{X}\bm{I}&\bm{0}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&(1-\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}+\mathcal{X}^{2})\bm{I}-2s\mathcal{X}\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\bm{J}&-2\textstyle{\sqrt{\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}}[s\mathcal{X}\bm{I}-(1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}})\bm{J}]\\[1.99997pt]
\bm{0}&-2\textstyle{\sqrt{\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}}}[s\mathcal{X}\bm{I}-(1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}})\bm{J}]&(1-\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}-\mathcal{X}^{2})\bm{I}+2s\mathcal{X}\bm{J}\end{pmatrix}\,\,,\,\,\mathrm{X}=(1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}})^{2}+\mathcal{X}^{2},

(87)

where we define
\mathcal{X}\vcentcolon=2\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}/\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}
. Applying the basis transformation
\mathbf{P}\mathbf{S}[0]\mathbf{P}^{\dagger}
where
\mathbf{P}=\bm{I}_{3}\otimes\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(\begin{smallmatrix}1&i\\
1&-i\end{smallmatrix}\right)
takes the scattering matrix to the creation and annihilation operator basis,
(\hat{a},\hat{a}^{\dagger},\hat{b},\hat{b}^{\dagger},\hat{c},\hat{c}^{\dagger})
. We can now extract the power gain in reflection off of mode B (in decibels) on resonance, defined as
\mathcal{G}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}(\mathrm{dB})\vcentcolon=10\log_{10}\left\lvert{\mathrm{S}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}[0]}\right\rvert^{2}
, where the scattering matrix element is
\hat{b}_{\mathrm{out}}=\mathrm{S}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}[0]\hat{b}_{\mathrm{in}}
. Given the same parameters, the gain (expressed as the original power ratio) is:

\mathcal{G}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}=\frac{\left[(1-\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}})(1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}})-\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}^{2}\right]^{2}\left[(1-\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}})^{2}+\mathcal{X}^{2}\right]}{\left(1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}-\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}\right)^{2}\left(1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}+\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}\right)^{2}\left[(1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}})^{2}+\mathcal{X}^{2}\right]},

(88)

The above expression goes to infinity as the critical stability condition in Eq. (
37
) is saturated, yielding the maximum measurement efficiency in Eq. (
40
). However, in certain parameter regimes, instability occurs for values of
\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}
well below the critical condition in Eq. (
37
) as a result of the 5
th
Hurwitz determinant going to zero before
\det[\bm{A}]
, such as in Fig.
10
. In this case, infinite gain cannot be realized.

C.3
Optimum measurement efficiency and its limits

From the main text, we are interested in the efficiency when
N^{(\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}},\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}})}=1
, requiring
\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}=\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}
and
\phi=-\pi/2
. Further assuming that
n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}},n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}=0
, the measurement induced dephasing in Eq. (
C.1
) reduces to

\Gamma_{\mathrm{d,m}}=\frac{8(\varepsilon^{2}/\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}})\mathcal{X}^{2}\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}(1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}})}{\left[(1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}})^{2}+\mathcal{X}^{2}\right]^{2}}.

(89)

For the measurement rate we can determine that the optimal amplifier phase is
\theta=-\pi/2+2\varphi
. For these parameters the ideal measurement operator for a measurement of the output of mode B is
\hat{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle M$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle M$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle M$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle M$}}}[0]=\cos(\varphi)\hat{q}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}},\mathrm{out}}[0]+\sin(\varphi)\hat{p}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}},\mathrm{out}}[0]
. The measurement rate is then:

\Gamma_{\mathrm{meas}}=\frac{8(\varepsilon^{2}/\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}})\mathcal{X}^{2}\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}^{2}}{\left[(1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}})^{2}+\mathcal{X}^{2}\right]^{2}}\times\frac{(-1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}+\mathcal{C}_{\lambda})^{2}}{4\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}(\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}+2n^{\mathrm{th}}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}})+(2\bar{n}_{\mathrm{rest}}+1)(1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}-\mathcal{C}_{\lambda})^{2}}.

(90)

As discussed in Sec.
V.2
, the approximate expression for the measurement efficiency given by
\eta\approx\Gamma_{\mathrm{meas}}/\Gamma_{\mathrm{d,m}}
in Eq. (
39
) is not valid for sufficiently large beam-splitter cooperativities. This is because one of the conditions for high efficiency from Sec.
II.3
,
\Gamma_{\mathrm{d,p}}\ll\Gamma_{\mathrm{d,m}}
, does not hold. Here we provide evidence to support this argument. In Fig.
10
, the parasitic and measurement-induced dephasing rates (for a fixed drive strength of
\varepsilon/2\pi=20
MHz) are plotted as functions of
\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}
and
\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}
.

Figure 10:
Qubit dephasing rates as a function of the unconstrained cooperativities
\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}
and
\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}
. The remaining cooperativities
\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}
and
\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}
are constrained in each simulation to optimize nonreciprocal scattering between A and C (see Eq. (
34
)) and retain
\mathcal{G}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}=20
dB, respectively. All other parameters are detailed in Sec.
V
. For
\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}\gtrsim 340
, the amplifier becomes unstable at the values of
\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}
required to retain
\mathcal{G}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}=20
dB.

As the cooperativity of the AB beam-splitter interaction
\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}
increases,
\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}
must be increased correspondingly to retain the optimal nonreciprocal scattering condition in Eq. (
34
). In addition, because
\mathcal{G}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}
monotonically decreases as
\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}
increases,
\mathcal{C}_{\lambda}
must also be increased to keep
\mathcal{G}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}
constant (at 20dB in Fig.
10
), until it is no longer possible to retain the gain in a stable amplifier configuration (which occurs at
\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}\gtrsim 340
for this system). This constraint results in a parasitic dephasing that grows monotonically with
\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{A}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}
, while the measurement induced dephasing remains constant, as shown in Eq. (
89
). Eventually,
\Gamma_{\mathrm{d,p}}
becomes a significant part of the total induced dephasing, reducing the efficiency.

Considering now the BC beam-splitter interaction, the limit on
\eta
in this case is set by a comparison of the dissipation rate and dispsersive shift of the dressed C mode, given by
\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}},\mathrm{eff}}\vcentcolon=\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}(1+\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}})
and
\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}},\mathrm{eff}}\approx\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}
, respectively. For qubit readout with a single mode,
\Gamma_{\mathrm{d,m}}
and
\Gamma_{\mathrm{meas}}
are maximized when the dispersive shift matches the mode’s dissipation rate,
\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}=\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}/2
. For the embedded amplifier, the equivalent condition is
\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}=\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}},\mathrm{eff}}/2
, requiring
\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}=2\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}/\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}-1\approx 49
. For constant values of
\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}
, larger values of
\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}
will therefore move the embedded amplifier measurement into the small-pull limit,
\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\ll\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}},\mathrm{eff}}/2
.
In this regime, the photon lifetime in mode C is short compared to the time required to attain substantial qubit information, eventually reducing
\Gamma_{\mathrm{d,m}}
, and hence
\Gamma_{\mathrm{meas}}
, to zero. While the parasitic dephasing peaks near the same cooperativity, it asymptotes to a nonzero value for large
\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}
. This is because
\Gamma_{\mathrm{d,p}}
is mainly dependent on
\mathcal{G}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}
, a quantity which becomes effectively independent of
\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}
when the beam-splitter rate is large. Values of
\mathcal{C}_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\gg 2\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}/\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}-1
therefore result in a larger contribution of
\Gamma_{\mathrm{d,p}}
to the total dephasing.

Appendix D
Experimental device calibrations

D.1
Preliminary characterization vs. SQUID bias

Here we discuss the preliminary measurements of the linear mode frequencies and linewidths as a function of flux bias. We note that for these preliminary measurements, we replace the band-pass Purcell filter and directional coupler of the experimental setup depicted in Fig.
22
with a single-stage circulator. We then measure the scattering parameter in reflection off of mode B to extract the B mode frequency and linewidth. Because the A and C mode frequencies lie outside the band of the circulators in the measurement setup, we extract their frequencies and linewidths by including an AB or BC beam-splitter interaction and fitting the scattering off of B using coupled-mode theory
Ranzani and Aumentado 2015
;
Lecocq et al. 2017
, which is equivalent to the scattering theory presented in Appendix
A.4
for these device configurations. These produce the measured linear mode frequencies and linewidths in Fig.
11
, which we fit with EM simulations presented in Appendix
G
. We note that both the A and C modes being outside the bandwidth of the measurement setup may account in part for the discrepancy in their measured and simulated linewidths. Changing to the standard measurement configuration shown in Fig.
22
, we extract the transmon’s
g\leftrightarrow e
transition frequency with a Ramsey sequence, and fit it with the same EM simulations, both of which are shown in Fig.
11
(a). Repeating these measurements as a function of dc SQUID flux bias informs the chosen operation point of
\Phi=0.246\Phi_{0}
. In the next section, we describe the method we employed to extract device parameters with a higher degree of accuracy at this chosen flux bias.

Figure 11:
Frequencies and linewidths vs. SQUID bias.
Measured and EM-simulated values for
(a)
the frequency of modes A, B, C and the qubit, and
(b)
the linewidth of modes A, B, and C, as a function of the flux bias through the dc SQUID. The dashed vertical line denotes the operation point of
\Phi=0.246\Phi_{0}
.

D.2
Catch-and-release protocol

As stated in the previous section, many static (linewidths, bare frequencies, dispersive shifts) and dynamic (beam-splitter coupling strengths, etc.) characteristics of a readout network can be calibrated with continuous-wave S or X parameter measurements paired with fits from a steady-state linear system model
Ranzani and Aumentado 2015
;
Lecocq et al. 2017
. However our chosen filtering and signal routing scheme (see Appendix
H
) allows only for measurements in reflection off of mode B, and also results in a large degree of Fano interference in this scattering parameter.

To avoid this issue, we employ pulsed drives and parametric interactions in a“catch-and-release” protocol, akin to those that have been demonstrated in other platforms
Novikova et al. 2007
;
Palomaki et al. 2013
;
Sirois et al. 2015
;
Peronnin et al. 2020
. These protocols not only remove continuous interference effects, but can also precisely calibrate device parameters. In this section, we analyze a simplified model of the protocol to demonstrate how it can extract the frequency and linewidth of mode
\mathrm{K}=\mathrm{A,C}
.

Here we consider a system consisting of a drive of strength
\varepsilon
and frequency
\omega_{d}
near mode B, and a beam-splitter pump with strength
g
and frequency
\omega_{p}
near
|\omega_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}-\omega_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}|
to couple mode K with mode B. Considering the evolution of the mean fields in the rotating frame
k\vcentcolon=\langle\hat{k}\rangle
,
b\vcentcolon=\langle\hat{b}\rangle
:

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}b

\displaystyle=\left(i\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}-\frac{\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}}{2}\right)b-igk+\varepsilon,

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}k

\displaystyle=\left(i\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}-\frac{\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}}{2}\right)k-igb,

(91)

where

\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}\vcentcolon=\omega_{d}-\omega_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}+s\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}.\qquad\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}\vcentcolon=\omega_{d}+\mathrm{sgn}(\omega_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}-\omega_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}})\omega_{p}-\omega_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}+s\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}.

(92)

In the above,
s=\braket{\sigma|\hat{\sigma}_{z}|\sigma}
takes the values
s=1
for
\sigma=e
, and
s=-1
for
\sigma=g
. Adiabatically eliminating mode B, we arrive at a single equation for K

\frac{d}{dt}k=\left(i\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}-\frac{\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}}{2}\right)k+\varepsilon_{\mathrm{eff}},

(93)

where

\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}\vcentcolon=\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}-\eta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}},\qquad\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}\vcentcolon=\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}+\eta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}},\qquad\varepsilon_{\mathrm{eff}}\vcentcolon=\varepsilon\sqrt{\eta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}},\qquad\eta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}\vcentcolon=\frac{g^{2}}{\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}^{2}/4+\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}^{2}}.

(94)

The catch-and-release model is as follows. The beam-splitter interaction and B drive are off for
t<0
, then both are concurrently turned on as a square pulse from time
t=0
to
t=t_{0}
. After these first pulses end there is a passive delay, followed by another square beam-splitter pulse beginning at
t=t_{1}
. The full procedure is visualized in Fig.
12
. The resulting solution of Eq. (
93
) yields a piecewise function:

k(t)=\frac{\varepsilon_{\mathrm{eff}}}{i\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}+\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}/2}\begin{cases}1-e^{(i\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}-\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}/2)t}&0<t<t_{0}\\
e^{(i\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}-\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}/2)(t-t_{0})}\left(1-e^{(i\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}-\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}/2)t_{0}}\right)&t_{0}<t<t_{1}\\
e^{(i\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}-\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}/2)(t-t_{1})}e^{(i\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}-\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}/2)(t_{1}-t_{0})}\left(1-e^{(i\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}-\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}/2)t_{0}}\right)&t>t_{1}\end{cases}

(95)

These equations describe an internal state
k(t)
that approaches its Lorentzian steady-state value for
0<t<t_{0}
, decays at a rate given by the bare mode linewidth
\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}
for
t_{0}<t<t_{1}
, and then decays at rate
\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}
for
t>t_{1}
. During this last step, energy is preferentially released out of the B mode to the monitored feedline (
\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}},\mathrm{ext}}\approx\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}
and
\eta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}\gg\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}
). Using input-output theory (see Appendix
A.4
), ignoring the prefactors independent of the evolution time
t_{\mathrm{ev}}\vcentcolon=t_{1}-t_{0}
and interrogation time
\tau\vcentcolon=t-t_{1}
, this output field is given by

b_{\mathrm{out}}(t_{\mathrm{ev}},\tau)=\sqrt{\eta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}}k(t)\propto e^{(i\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}-\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}/2)\tau}e^{(i\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}-\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}/2)t_{\mathrm{ev}}}.

(96)

We linearly amplify this field, and conduct homodyne detection by first sending the signal to the RF port of an IQ mixer. For the sake of simplicity, for this analysis we set the LO frequency to the original B drive frequency
\omega_{d}
. In the frame of the downconverted signal, and ignoring fast-oscillating sidebands, the voltages produced at the I and Q ports of the mixer can be expressed as
Blais et al. 2021
:

V(t_{\mathrm{ev}},\tau)\vcentcolon=V_{I}(t_{\mathrm{ev}},\tau)+iV_{Q}(t_{\mathrm{ev}},\tau)\propto b_{\mathrm{out}}(t_{\mathrm{ev}},\tau).

(97)

To produce a set of IQ coordinates for each value of
t_{\mathrm{ev}}
, we perform a weighted integration of these voltages with respect to
\tau
.

z_{\mathrm{IQ}}(t_{\mathrm{ev}})\vcentcolon=I(t_{\mathrm{ev}})+iQ(t_{\mathrm{ev}})=\int_{0}^{\tau^{\prime}}w^{*}(\tau)V(t_{\mathrm{ev}},\tau)d\tau.

(98)

Assuming the signal is on top of white noise, the weighting function
w(\tau)
that maximizes SNR contains the same amplitude envelope and oscillation frequency as these output voltages with respect to the interrogation time
Bultink et al. 2018
:

w(\tau)=e^{(i\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}-\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}/2)\tau}.

(99)

Averaging the weight-biased signal over a long duration
\tau^{\prime}\gg 2\pi/\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}},\mathrm{eff}}
so that the upper integration bound can be extended
\tau^{\prime}\rightarrow\infty
produces

\displaystyle z_{\mathrm{IQ}}(t_{\mathrm{ev}})\propto e^{(i\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}+\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}/2)t_{\mathrm{ev}}}.

(100)

We note that this proportional relationship holds regardless of the weight function used in the integration. Due to the adiabatic elimination of mode B and the exclusion of the upper sidebands in this simple model, the experimentally-observed voltages at the I and Q ports of the mixer as a function of
\tau
will in general differ from the simple exponential form given in Eq. (
96
). This merely changes the form of the optimal weight function employed in the time-averaging, and does not affect the dynamics of the system during
t_{\mathrm{ev}}
. A treatment of a similar experiment without the adiabatic elimination of the buffer mode is performed in Ref.
Peronnin et al. 2020
. Moving forward, it is instructive to write the amplitude and phase of the demodulated shot-averaged signal
z_{\mathrm{IQ}}(t_{\mathrm{ev}})
:

|z_{\mathrm{IQ}}(t_{\mathrm{ev}})|\propto e^{-\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}t_{\mathrm{ev}}/2},\qquad\arg(z_{\mathrm{IQ}}(t_{\mathrm{ev}}))=\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}t_{\mathrm{ev}}.

(101)

Therefore, the bare K mode linewidth
\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}
and detuning
\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}
can be directly extracted by fitting the resulting exponential amplitude decay and linear phase accumulation of the demodulated and shot-averaged IQ coordinates with respect to
t_{\mathrm{ev}}
. This is apparent in the application of the catch-and-release protocol to modes
\mathrm{K}=\mathrm{A},\mathrm{C}
in Fig.
12
. To extract the dispersive shift, this protocol can be performed for each qubit state. We can then use Eq. (
92
) to compute the bare mode frequency
\omega_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}
and dispersive shift
\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}
:

\displaystyle\omega_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}

\displaystyle=\omega_{d}+\mathrm{sgn}(\omega_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}-\omega_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}})\omega_{p}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}(s=1)+\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}(s=-1)\right),

(102)

\displaystyle\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left(\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}(s=1)-\Delta_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}(s=-1)\right).

(103)

It is worth emphasizing that these expressions only hold for
g_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{C}$}}}\ll\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{B}$}}}
.

D.3
Catch and release: A and C mode characterization

Here we utilize the catch-and-release protocol described above to find the frequencies, dispersive shifts, and linewidths of the amplifier and cavity modes (
\omega_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}
,
\chi_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}
, and
\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}
, where
\mathrm{K}=\mathrm{A},\mathrm{C}
). We note that in general, the frequency shift due to the dispersive interaction also changes the environment that the mode sees, and thus the linewidth. The linewidths quoted in the main body (i.e. Fig.
3
(c)) are the averaged values with respect to qubit state
(\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}(s=1)+\gamma_{\mathchoice{\scalebox{0.8}{$\displaystyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\textstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}{\scalebox{0.8}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathrm{K}$}}}(s=-1))/2
.

In the experimental implementation, the duration of the initial beam-splitter and B drive is
t_{0}=60
ns including 10 ns
\cos^{2}
rise and fall envelopes. We keep the drive weak (
|\varepsilon|=4.6
kHz) to avoid contributions to the dynamics from the A or C mode’s unknown self-Kerr nonlinearity, an effect which can appear as an inflated amplitude decay rate in the demodulated and shot-averaged IQ data. We sweep the idling time up to
t_{\mathrm{ev}}=10

\mu
s and probe the released signal for
\tau=300
ns. We construct the weighted averaging functions from shot-averaged (
5\times 10^{5}
shots for the A mode,
10^{6}
shots for the C mode) time-traces of the downconverted signal with the qubit in the ground state. We then repeat this procedure for both states of the qubit, whose measurement and reset protocol is detailed in Appendix
E
. Fig.
12
shows the simplified experimental sequence and resulting data for modes A and C, demonstrating the exponential decay in amplitude and linear accumulation of phase with respect to
t_{\mathrm{ev}}
. We fit the data to Eq. (
100
) and Eq. (
101
), then utilize Eqs. (
102
) and (
103
) to obtain the results tabulated in Table
1
.

mode

\pm

\pm

\pm
